The Heukryong Secret Armor was an excellent choice. And that was not all. From wrist guards to a waistband with pouches, knee guards, elbow guards, and even gauntlets for those who practiced fist techniques—he was wearing everything that could be strapped to the body.

“Because Father told me to dress properly.”

At that, Geom Ujin stared intently at Geom Mugeuk and said,

“I didn’t tell you to wear four layers, did I?”

For a moment, Geom Mugeuk flinched.

“Surely not? Have you mastered some demonic art that lets you converse with armors?”

“Need I learn such an art? Can’t one tell just by looking in the mirror?”

Geom Mugeuk stood before the mirror. His body was thicker than usual. Anyone could see he was layered up.

“It’s a bit obvious.”

Indeed, Geom Mugeuk was wearing four layers of armor atop one another.

“Who are you planning to give them to?”

“These precious items, how could I have no one to give them to? Ah, and as Father ordered, I intended to wear this Heukryong Secret Armor myself. I shall also wear these Ssangrang (Twin Wolves).”

The black Heukryong Secret Armor bore the image of a black dragon upon closer inspection, and the Ssangrang wrist guards bore splendid images of wolves.

“And I gave up on looking stylish to wear these.”

As Geom Mugeuk made a pitiful face and tried to remove the armors, Geom Ujin spoke.

“Enough. Since you’ve already put them on, take them.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk’s expression brightened.

“As expected! My father is the greatest!”

Geom Mugeuk removed the layers of armors and protective gear. Looking at the armors that came out from underneath, he had chosen only the best even then.

Heukryong Secret Armor and the Ssangrang he wore himself; the rest he folded neatly and kept.

Geom Ujin watched his son’s antics in silence. He also pretended not to see him discreetly tucking the Hyeolpung Gwanghwi Sword among the armors.

Having packed away the armors, Geom Mugeuk now brought out martial robes he had set aside separately.

“Here, put these martial robes on over your armor.”

Wherever he had found them, they were martial robes made of Cheonjamsa. They were not as great a treasure as supreme-grade Cheonjamsa, but they were of incomparably finer quality than ordinary martial robes.

They were lighter than ordinary martial robes, yet incomparably tougher, and allowed air to pass through well.

He had given the best to his father, and the next best to Hwi.

“I don’t like that color.”

Hwi thought inwardly,

‘You need not do this. I have already lost to the Young Lord.’

Thus, the three of them dressed properly and stood side by side before the mirror. Though the colors differed, because they wore martial robes of the same design, they looked like fellow disciples from the same sect.

Of course, Geom Mugeuk had not made his father his master.

“From now on, I shall call you senior brothers. Since Father looks younger, you shall be Second Senior Brother, and Uncle Hwi shall be Eldest Senior Brother. Senior brothers! Let us sally forth to the most bustling main street of Junggyeong. The young ladies will line up for us.”

Then Geom Mugeuk saw. The two of them looking at him in the mirror with different smiles.

‘Yes, smile like that. Live while smiling like that. I will keep making you laugh. Even if the jokes are lame, even if my antics overstep, I will keep trying. Because time flows on even as we laugh. There is no time to frown.’

His father strode toward the cave entrance and gave Hwi an order.

“Come back later and move everything remaining here to the Cheonma Treasury.”

* * *

The smell of blood wafted from the entrance.

Jin Hagun, the Myeolmadaeju, who had entered the manor, furrowed his brow. That the smell of blood was discernible even from outside meant that many people had died.

The warriors of the Martial Alliance guarding the entrance respectfully bowed their heads to Jin Hagun.

Jin Hagun entered the manor grounds with his Demon Extermination warriors in tow.

The middle-aged warrior waiting in the courtyard was Ju Gwol, the Junggyeong Branch Leader of the Martial Alliance.

“Welcome, Commander Jin.”

“Branch Leader Ju, it has been a long time.”

Ju Gwol’s attitude toward Jin Hagun was extremely reverent. Though not yet formally appointed as successor, it was considered a foregone conclusion that Jin Hagun would become the next Alliance Leader. As the Myeolmadaeju, he had steadily built his reputation through successive exploits.

“What on earth happened?”

“An incident occurred here at Yumyeong Manor, so I urgently requested your assistance.”

Normally, no matter what incident occurred, it was standard for the Junggyeong Branch to handle it first. The fact that he had been called from the start meant it was a matter of such importance and severity.

“Come, let us enter.”

Ju Gwol led Jin Hagun toward the building. The closer they drew, the thicker the stench of blood became.

“Yumyeong Manor is a renowned sect here in Junggyeong. Its master was a man who valued righteousness, receiving the respect of all martial artists in Junggyeong.”

Upon entering the building, the interior was nothing short of a bloodbath. Blood was splattered everywhere.

“It appears they were ambushed.”

A trail of blood stretched along the corridor.

It was clear that Ju Gwol’s handling of affairs differed from usual.

The fact that he said it was an ambush meant that the culprit was still unknown.

Normally, the Demon Extermination Corps was requested when the culprit was identified but was too powerful for branch forces to handle. Yet now, he had been called even though the culprit was unknown. Why?

And the reason became clear the moment they opened the doors and entered the main hall.

That place, too, was covered in blood.

Jin Hagun’s gaze fixed on the rear wall. Words written in blood were there.

As he beheld them, Jin Hagun’s eyes went wide.

“The Demonic Way Under Heaven.”

If one were to ask what four characters written in blood on a wall could most startle the Myeolmadaeju, these four would be the answer.

Jin Hagun stared at the blood-written characters and asked,

“Where are the bodies of those slain at Yumyeong Manor?”

He needed to check immediately whether they had been struck by demonic arts.

“There are no bodies.”

Jin Hagun, who had been looking at the characters, quickly turned to Ju Gwol.

“No bodies?”

“Yes, not a single body was found. However, judging by the amount of blood left, it seems impossible that they could be alive.”

Jin Hagun’s gaze turned back to the wall.

“Who would commit such a bizarre act of taking away the bodies they killed? It is undoubtedly the work of the demonic cult bastards.”

At Ju Gwol’s definitive statement, Jin Hagun replied in a calm tone,

“If your name were written there, would it mean you did it, Branch Leader?”

Ju Gwol was inwardly flustered. He had assumed the Myeolmadaeju would also take it as the work of the demonic cult.

“But who would dare impersonate the demonic cult?”

“There have been past incidents of demonic cult impersonation. Do not jump to conclusions.”

Ju Gwol pressed his opinion no further.

“I was rash.”

Whether it was the work of a true demonic practitioner or someone framing the demonic cult, this incident was not something to be overlooked.

Jin Hagun spoke to Ju Gwol.

“Declare a state of emergency immediately and deploy all branch warriors to this case. Request cooperation from all neighboring branches and sub-branches, and thoroughly investigate anyone leaving Junggyeong.”

“I shall execute it at once.”

Having received the order, Ju Gwal ran outside.

Jin Hagun let out a light sigh and looked again at the characters written on the wall.

“The Demonic Way Under Heaven.”

At that, a certain person naturally came to mind. Because when one heard the words “demonic way,” there was a man who naturally came to mind.

‘Your demonic way is being stained with blood.’

‘Where are you now?’

* * *

The carriage continued southward.

“Ah, this means farewell to Junggyeong as well.”

A little further, and the carriage would leave Junggyeong.

“Are you really not going? Let’s stay and play for just four days. The women of Junggyeong are said to be very beautiful.”

Then Geom Ujin spoke quietly.

“Marshal Sima is waiting.”

His father, who had ignored all the silly jokes, had actually replied for once. Geom Mugeuk wasn’t about to let this opportunity pass.

“That only shows you don’t know the Marshal’s heart. Marshal Sima must be relishing this rare freedom. ‘Under one, above ten thousand’ may be nice, but wouldn’t he sometimes want to take out the ‘under one’ and just enjoy being ‘above ten thousand’? Every morning, stretching in the front courtyard of the Cheonma Hall, saying, ‘The Lord isn’t here, wonderful! Lord, please stay and play three more days in Junggyeong!’ That’s what he must be thinking!”

As if imagining such a Sima Myeong, a faint smile formed at the corner of Geom Ujin’s lips.

Just then, the carriage slowly came to a halt.

“Oh! So only Uncle Hwi understands my heart! Are we turning the carriage around?”

Of course, was that why Hwi had stopped the carriage?

“Up ahead, martial warriors are controlling the road.”

At Hwi’s words, Geom Mugeuk leaned his head out to look, and saw carriages and people lined up on the road.

In the distance, Martial Alliance warriors were inspecting travelers.

They numbered not four or five but several dozen, indicating this was no ordinary inspection.

At that moment, a warrior from the Tongcheonggak, disguised as a traveler, secretly passed a folded slip of paper to Hwi and disappeared into the crowd.

Hwi delivered the message to Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk unfolded the paper and checked its contents. The playfulness vanished from his face in an instant.

“An incident has occurred. All members of Yumyeong Manor, a righteous sect of Junggyeong, have vanished, and the words ‘The Demonic Way Under Heaven’ were left on the wall in blood.”

Then Geom Mugeuk saw. A cold glint in his father’s eyes.

Whoever it was, they had left behind words that should never have been left.

Soon, Geom Ujin quietly closed his eyes and spoke softly.

“Turn the carriage around.”

Absolute Regression: Chapter 496

Chapter 496: You May Not Believe It, But I Was Just Passing By
--------------------------------------------------------------
Hwi turned the carriage around.

The carriage that had been trying to leave Zhongjing was now heading back toward the center of Zhongjing.

"Let us head to the safe house first."

His father did not say a single word on the way there.

Geom Mugeuk also remained silent. It was not a situation for jokes or idle chatter. He could clearly feel his father's fury.

His father was not one to show emotion toward enemies. It had been the same when facing the assassins earlier. Though he despised assassins, he had not shown such feelings particularly while dealing with the Slaughter King.

But this time was different. Had it merely been news of someone impersonating the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, his father would have suggested returning to the main cult immediately.

But the problem was the message left behind.

The Demonic Way Under Heaven.

They had uttered words that should never be spoken lightly. Moreover, these days his father must be contemplating many things between his own Demonic Path and his son's Demonic Path.

The place where the carriage arrived was a special location among Zhongjing's many safe houses. The safest of places, reachable only by passing through a hidden path and formations—it was the Cult Leader's private safe house.

Upon arrival, Geom Ujin went straight to his quarters.

Geom Mugeuk helped Hwi unload the luggage from the carriage.

"Please go inside. I will do this."

"No, I should carry my own things."

Geom Mugeuk, together with Hwi, moved his luggage and the artifacts brought from the treasury into the room.

He packed them away neatly, but continued wearing the Black Dragon armor and the Twin Wolves he had donned earlier.

He had defied his father's words to choose items for himself, instead taking several sets to give to others. Out of apology for that, he intended to continue wearing these until they returned to the main cult.

After unpacking in this manner, Geom Mugeuk went straight to his father's room.

In the meantime, a new report sent by the Tongcheon Pavilion had arrived. Geom Mugeuk read it, having already been checked by his father.

"It seems the Martial Alliance is considering casting a massive dragnet centered on Zhongjing."

Casting such a dragnet was no simple matter. Countless martial artists had to be mobilized, and it required enormous expense.

Furthermore, it was an act that would touch the nerves of the other two great powers. With so many troops and experts on the move, the fact that they were rushing to consider a dragnet meant—

"That they consider this matter to be of utmost importance."

At Geom Mugeuk's words, Geom Ujin nodded.

"What do you think of this matter?"

At his father's question, Geom Mugeuk conveyed what he had been thinking during the carriage ride.

"As you know, there have always been those who slander and impersonate our main cult. Yet this case leaves me with some concerns."

"And what is that?"

Geom Mugeuk cocked his head and replied as if to himself.

"Why of all places Yumyeong Manor?"

Had he been thinking the same thing? Or had Geom Mugeuk touched upon something he had not considered? A strange gleam flickered across his father's eyes.

"I do not know much about Yumyeong Manor, but I do know that the master of Yumyeong Manor is deeply respected by the Righteous faction martial artists of Zhongjing."

Though Yumyeong Manor was no more than a small-to-medium-sized sect, its master's renown far exceeded its scale. It was because he always led the way in acts of chivalry rather than expanding his own influence.

"Therefore, to strike at the master of Yumyeong Manor in Zhongjing is also to attack the entire Righteous faction."

That was why talk of a dragnet was already surfacing. The Martial Alliance had to catch the culprit behind this incident, and they intended to do so.

Even amid the Martial Alliance's urgent situation, his father did not rush.

His father was one who had led the main cult alongside Military Adviser Sima for a long time. He knew better than anyone that moving hastily in anger meant dancing to the enemy's tune.

His father called Hwi and gave an order.

"Place all branches and sub-branches in Zhongjing on high alert, and order the Tongcheon Pavilion to focus its entire intelligence network on this matter."

"Yes, understood."

His father's fury was cold.

And Geom Mugeuk knew. That this coldness held molten lava within.

Those who withstood this coldness and melted it away would instead face his true rage then.

* * *

That evening, Father, I, and Hwi were dining at the largest inn on the main street.

Since ancient times, inns have been the source of all rumors in the world. Thus, at times, they are places where one can obtain information more useful than that bought from information dealers at great cost.

We took our seats and ordered simple dishes and drink, and indeed, the surroundings were filled with nothing but talk of Yumyeong Manor.

Most conversations started with longing for the chivalrous acts the master of Yumyeong Manor had performed in life, only to end with curses toward the damned Demonic Cult.

Amidst the ordinary chatter going around the table, Geom Mugeuk focused on one fact.

"The rumors are spreading too quickly."

"Of course, given the master of Yumyeong Manor's reputation in Zhongjing, it is natural for this to be the center of attention. But the problem is this."

"The Martial Alliance would have kept the words left on the wall as secret as possible while investigating this incident."

"Because it involved the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, they would have handled it with utmost caution."

"Yet for rumors to have spread this far already means there is someone deliberately spreading them. Very intentionally and methodically."

Then, Hwi asked cautiously. It was unlike him to ever intervene in conversations between the two, but as the captain of the guards, he had to confirm this much.

"Could it be that they are targeting the Cult Leader?"

Before answering, Geom Mugeuk looked at his father, who was tilting his cup in silence. Though seated comfortably, there was not a single opening to be found.

The fact that he could find no openings even with his own martial prowess meant that there were none to begin with, did it not?

Soon after, Geom Mugeuk answered Hwi.

"If it were such a reckless fool, it would be easier to deal with."

This matter was a far more calculated conspiracy than that.

"My instincts tell me this will not end with just this one incident."

And that premonition became reality far faster than Geom Mugeuk had imagined.

A man entered the inn and shouted to everyone.

"This time, it is the Daejeong Gate that has been attacked by the Demonic Cult bastards!"

The people in the inn erupted into commotion all at once, and the hall grew noisy. Daejeong Gate was also one of the orthodox sects symbolizing the Righteous faction in Zhongjing.

Enraged by someone's shout that the Demonic Cult must be destroyed, the gathered heroes cried out together.

The enemy's intent was clear. They were trying to incite conflict between the main cult and the Righteous faction.

Geom Mugeuk spoke cautiously to his father.

"Should we not send word to the Martial Alliance?"

He meant that his father should personally inform the Martial Alliance Leader that this was not their doing.

At that, Geom Ujin emptied his cup and rose from his seat.

"We must verify things first."

* * *

Jin Hagun entered Daejeong Gate with a stiff face.

Though the perimeter was thoroughly controlled by Martial Alliance martial artists, nothing could block the stench of blood wafting from within. Already, Righteous faction martial artists from Zhongjing were gathering after hearing the rumors.

The rumors were spreading far too quickly. That meant the nerves of Zhongjing's martial artists were on edge.

Jin Hagun stopped in his tracks after entering the building.

A deep sigh escaped him unconsciously.

The scene before his eyes was truly a massacre.

Daejeong Gate martial artists lay dead, their bodies torn and slashed, a miserable sight.

At Yumyeong Manor earlier, no bodies had been found. Thus, he had harbored hope that perhaps they had survived. Seeing these corpses, that hope vanished.

Jin Hagun carefully passed between the corpses and entered the sect leader's chamber.

The Lord of Daejeong Gate was dead, leaning against the wall. His chest had been completely torn open; he had died so horribly that one could hardly bear to look.

And above the corpse, characters carved in blood were written in large letters.

The Demonic Way Under Heaven.

The same words left at Yumyeong Manor.

As Jin Hagun glared at it in silence, Ju Gwol, the head of the Zhongjing branch, entered.

"They killed every last person belonging to Daejeong Gate. Even the women who cleaned and the cooks who prepared meals in the kitchen were all killed."

Jin Hagun clenched his fist and gritted his teeth.

Even for those who lived by killing, there was a minimum line that must not be crossed. Even if one held a grudge, one should only kill the person with whom one had the grievance. What happened here today crossed that line.

'I will never forgive them.'

This was exactly why they existed—to prevent such incidents and to punish those who committed them.

Ju Gwol, standing beside him, wanted to ask if this still wasn't the work of the Demonic Cult, but he held his tongue.

"Master Seong is arriving now."

Master Seong was a demonic arts researcher of the Martial Alliance who had spent his life studying demonic arts.

He was a figure more versed in demonic arts than anyone; a man who could determine which demonic art had killed a person based on bloodstains at the scene, traces of combat, and wounds on the corpses.

Ju Gwol intended to endure until Master Seong arrived and presented his results. For even after seeing the characters left on the wall, this Demon Annihilation Commander refused to believe it was the work of the Demonic Cult.

'He can't possibly doubt even Master Seong's findings.'

Had Jin Hagun read Ju Gwol's mind?

"Had this been a few years ago, I too would have thought it the work of the Demonic Cult, like Branch Leader Ju."

"What has changed since then?"

Jin Hagun, looking at the characters written in blood, turned to Ju Gwol and answered.

"I have changed."

Ju Gwol understood the meaning behind those words. It was something he would not normally say, but now that two Righteous sects in Zhongjing had been attacked in succession, Ju Gwol spoke what he had been holding back.

"I have heard of the relationship between the Commander and the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult."

For the figure most at the center of attention recently was none other than the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult.

He had also heard rumors that among the high-ranking figures of the Martial Alliance, there were words of concern regarding the friendship between Geom Mugeuk and Jin Hagun.

"You must not trust the Demonic Cult."

After a brief pause, Jin Hagun answered.

"I do not trust the Demonic Cult either."

Two thoughts rose simultaneously in Jin Hagun's mind.

'However, as long as he is the Young Cult Leader, the Demonic Cult would never do such a thing.'

The next thought.

'But what if this truly is the work of the Demonic Cult? What if everything was merely an act to hide such deeds?'

Considering his relationship with Geom Mugeuk until now, the second thought was a suspicion he should never have. He knew he shouldn't think it. He also knew how sorry he would feel toward Geom Mugeuk later.

But faced with the horrific corpse of the Lord of Daejeong Gate and the words "The Demonic Way Under Heaven" written in blood, he couldn't help but think it.

It was at that very moment.

"That hurts. I trusted you."

At the voice from behind, Jin Hagun and Ju Gwol turned around in shock.

Without anyone noticing, Geom Mugeuk was standing at the entrance.

Jin Hagun was so startled he froze, unable to say a word. He had never dreamed he would see Geom Mugeuk here today. Moreover, he had just been thinking of him, which made it even more shocking.

Ju Gwol drew his sword and shouted.

"Intruder!"

Dozens of subordinates were deployed outside, yet someone had sneaked in here. Were they merely branch martial artists? There were also Demon Annihilation Corps martial artists led by Jin Hagun.

'How did he get here?'

At Ju Gwol's cry, martial artists swarmed in. They too were surprised and bewildered to see Geom Mugeuk, who had entered right under their noses.

Then, Jin Hagun spoke to everyone.

"He is someone I know. Sheathe your swords. All of you, withdraw."

Jin Hagun dismissed all the martial artists.

Ju Gwol sheathed his sword and recalled a certain person.

'Could it be?'

As Ju Gwol had expected.

"It's been a while, friend."

These days, the only person who could call Jin Hagun a friend was that very person from the rumors.

'So this is the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult!'

Ju Gwol's heart trembled. Today was the first time he had seen the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult.

Jin Hagun had no leisure to exchange relaxed greetings.

"What brings you here?"

To Jin Hagun's question, Geom Mugeuk let out a light sigh and answered.

"You won't believe it, but I stopped by on my way through."

Because something had truly happened just as he was passing through Zhongjing.

Ju Gwol's expression soured. Crazy! He just stopped by on his way through?

With the Young Cult Leader appearing here, Ju Gwol became even more convinced that this was the work of the Demonic Cult.

On the other hand, Jin Hagun was only now truly feeling that he had met Geom Mugeuk. Geom Mugeuk was someone who gave such absurd answers. And those absurd words were all sincere and true.

Frankly, he was glad to see Geom Mugeuk. But he could not show that joy in this situation. For his heart was now filled with rage toward the perpetrator and sorrow for the dead.

Given the situation, Geom Mugeuk clarified that he had truly been passing by, rather than speaking frivolously.

"You will know if you investigate. I received help from your Shaanxi Branch, after all. I am on my way back to the main cult after handling affairs there."

Fortunately, he had received help from the Shaanxi Branch while handling the matter of the Slaughter King. If they investigated his route and timing, it would somewhat explain why he was here.

Geom Mugeuk slowly walked over to where the two were standing.

Ju Gwol was inwardly on guard, gathering his internal energy to strike at any moment.

'Truly, I cannot feel even a hint of demonic energy.'

He was astonished that a Demonic Cult member could hide his demonic energy so thoroughly. He felt as if Geom Mugeuk knew no martial arts at all.

It truly seemed as if he were the perfected image of Returning to True Self.

Geom Mugeuk stood before Jin Hagun. Their gazes intertwined in the air.

"If I am this angry, I cannot even imagine how furious you must be."

At Geom Mugeuk's consolation, Jin Hagun revealed his resolve.

"I will never forgive whoever did this. Even if, by some chance, it were you."

"You should. I intend to do the same."

Geom Mugeuk's gaze turned toward the characters written on the wall.

"The one who left those words has no idea what my father's and my Demonic Path truly is."

Jin Hagun had also thought that this wasn't the work of the Demonic Cult, if only because of the words left behind.

The Heavenly Demon Arts he had witnessed at the tripartite meeting, and the Geom Mugeuk he knew—they were not ones who would kill and leave such words behind.

'Yes, this man didn't do it. Because he is not someone who would do such a thing.'

Just then, a subordinate's voice was heard from the entrance.

"Master Seong has arrived."

They turned to see a white-haired old man entering.

Absolute Regression, Chapter 497.

Chapter 497: Things That Must Not Be Touched.
---------------------------------------------
Master Seong was an old man with white hair, but his back was straight, and the light in his eyes was intense.

Moreover, the neatly worn black jangsam contrasted with his white hair, creating a strange air about him.

He was carrying a large box, and from beneath the white cloth covering it protruded various tools of unknown purpose.

The first to greet him was Jin Hagun.

“It has been a long time.”

Jin Hagun had known Master Seong since he was young. Master Seong was an indispensable figure in the Murim Alliance when it came to matters related to demonic arts, and his research into demonic arts was acknowledged by all.

From the Murim Alliance’s standpoint, he was someone who studied the martial arts of their most powerful enemy, so how could they not value him? Even in his old age, he remained active, training numerous disciples.

“Have you been well, Captain Jin? I’ve heard much of your accomplishments as captain of the Demon-Extermination Unit.”

Jin Hagun introduced Geom Mugeuk to him.

“This is the Young Cult Leader of the Divine Cult.”

Master Seong was startled, not having expected the Demon Cult’s Young Cult Leader to be here. He examined Geom Mugeuk with a renewed look in his eyes.

“You are quite different from what I imagined.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled as he greeted him.

“I have long heard of your reputation, sir.”

“You people must dislike me, no?”

“How could that be? On a day like this, it is precisely because you are here, Master, that we may be cleared of false charges.”

Master Seong’s eyes narrowed.

“We will know once I investigate.”

Ju Gwol, who had brought Master Seong, made a polite request.

“As you know, this is a grave matter. The truth of this incident rests solely in your hands.”

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk said to Ju Gwol,

“Do you hope the culprit is us?”

Ju Gwol thought that perhaps he did. If someone other than the Demon Cult had committed such a brutal act, then that would be far more terrible than the culprit being from the Demon Cult.

Ju Gwol once again made a courteous request of Master Seong.

“I leave it in your hands.”

It was a plea laden with the wish that he would not miss it if it was demonic art.

“No matter what you all wish for, I will only reveal things as they are.”

Master Seong walked over to the corpse of the Daejeong Sect Master.

He opened the box, took out his tools, and began his investigation in earnest.

He collected blood from the corpse and mixed it with the liquid in a medicine bottle.

He examined the wounds carefully, and applied different medicines to the severed flesh, bones, and organs.

Nor did he examine only the corpse. He investigated where the blood had splattered from and to, and what traces had been left on the walls.

He scattered powdered medicine on the floor to reveal footprints, determining what footwork had been used here. He conducted every investigation he could.

Unless a technique had been used that left behind a very distinctive trace, even this made it difficult to determine exactly what martial art had been employed. There were simply too many similar martial arts in this murim.

After watching him for a while, Geom Mugeuk and Jin Hagun walked over to the window.

Ju Gwol could not follow them and watched from afar. He found it displeasing that, in this situation, Jin Hagun was associating with the Demon Cult’s Young Cult Leader.

Geom Mugeuk and Jin Hagun silently looked out the window. The Murim Alliance warriors were moving the corpses into the courtyard.

Jin Hagun saw it. The look in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes as he watched that sight was no different from his own. He, too, was as furious and sorrowful as Jin Hagun was.

Knowing that he was this kind of person, the fact that he had suspected Geom Mugeuk earlier might have been due to his own personality.

He was someone who did not truly trust others. No, perhaps he was someone who did not know how to trust.

“Why did you go to Shaanxi?”

At Jin Hagun’s question, Geom Mugeuk answered honestly.

“The Tongcheongak of our cult discovered the funding line of the ones behind this in Shaanxi. I went to cut off that money line.”

Jin Hagun looked at Geom Mugeuk.

“Did you cut it?”

“Clean through.”

A faint smile appeared at the corner of Jin Hagun’s mouth, then vanished.

“Do you think they’re the ones who did this as well?”

“They could be. Or they could not be.”

Taking revenge for the elimination of the King of Assassins?

If so? Then those who had been hiding and weaving plots until now would be making their first proactive attack.

But the King of Assassins had died only a short while ago. Would they carry out revenge this quickly? This incident seemed to have been prepared meticulously. Would they really have had time to prepare in the interim?

Geom Mugeuk sent a voice transmission to Jin Hagun.

—My father came out with me.

Jin Hagun looked at Geom Mugeuk in surprise.

—I’m telling you because I don’t want to deceive you.

—Where is he now?

—At a safe house of our cult.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Jin Hagun.

—That is the proof. Proof that we did not do this. Would I commit such an act while my father is with me?

Jin Hagun now believed him completely. He knew how much Geom Mugeuk liked and respected the Heavenly Demon. Even if he were to use some kind of deception, he was not someone who would do so while invoking his father.

—Thank you for telling me.

—When we become Cult Leader and Alliance Leader, we may face something like this again. When that time comes, you tell me too. Honestly, like I did now. Then no matter what attempts to drive us apart, no matter what misunderstandings arise, we will be able to overcome them.

The two looked at each other, then turned their gazes back to the courtyard. The corpses continued to be moved.

In the meantime, Master Seong’s investigation continued. His investigation was meticulous and fervent.

He did not frown even once at the foul-smelling medicines. He lay prone on the floor where blood had pooled, searching for something. He connected red threads here and there throughout the hall, trying to recreate the fight that had taken place here.

How much time passed like that?

At last, Master Seong finished his investigation.

Everyone gathered around Master Seong. Amid the tense gazes focused on him, Master Seong spoke calmly.

“I have determined what martial art it was.”

Jin Hagun and Ju Gwol, of course, and even Geom Mugeuk waited for his answer with tense expressions.

“The martial art that killed the Daejeong Sect Master was…”

Master Seong’s gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk.

“It was demonic art.”

Jin Hagun flinched in shock, while Ju Gwol wore an expression that seemed to say, As expected.

If anything, Geom Mugeuk remained calm.

This conspiracy had truly been deliberate. Though he had told Master Seong that he would clear them of false charges, he had anticipated that the culprit might have killed using demonic art.

Ju Gwol asked Master Seong,

“Were you able to determine specifically what demonic art it was?”

To his surprise, Master Seong nodded.

A shocking truth flowed from his mouth.

“It was the demonic art of a Demon Venerable.”

All three who were listening were shocked.

This time, Geom Mugeuk, who had been calm earlier, was the most surprised. He truly had not expected a Demon Venerable’s demonic art to appear.

Even Ju Gwol, who had insisted it was demonic art, grew taut with tension when a Demon Venerable was mentioned. The culprit was a Demon Venerable? This was an issue on a completely different level from anything so far.

“Who is it? Which Demon Venerable?”

At Ju Gwol’s trembling question, Master Seong identified one person.

“Hyeolcheon Doma.”

“!”

“The one that killed the Daejeong Sect Master was his Myeolcheon Demon Saber Form.”

A heavy silence descended over the place.

Hyeolcheon Doma had massacred Yuming Manor and the Daejeong Sect.

If this news spread through the jianghu, not only the Chongqing murim but the entire murim would be turned upside down. Even if war broke out between the Murim Alliance and the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, it would not be strange at all for this incident to be the spark.

Jin Hagun looked at Geom Mugeuk. Geom Mugeuk was silently staring at Master Seong.

Taking that reaction as an admission of Master Seong’s words, Ju Gwol swiftly launched himself toward the entrance. Then he called in all his subordinates outside. Geom Mugeuk paid him no attention.

The first thought that came to him after hearing Master Seong’s words was this.

‘Could this Master Seong also be someone recruited by Hwa Mugi’s side?’

Because he had produced such an absurd result.

But in his life before regression, Master Seong had never appeared in connection with the Twelve Zodiac Kings.

He could have helped them from the shadows, but that could not have been the case. The Twelve Zodiac Kings had appeared after the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult closed its gates, and Master Seong of that time had been unnecessary.

Moreover, there was one more thing.

If he had been recruited by them, he would not have produced such a result in front of him, knowing he would be immediately suspected.

He would have said it was another martial art here, then gone to the Murim Alliance Leader and sowed discord. Saying something like, I could not speak the truth in front of the Young Cult Leader.

‘This man is not their pawn.’

That was why this result was astonishing and shocking to Geom Mugeuk.

Jin Hagun confirmed with Master Seong once more.

“Are you certain it was Hyeolcheon Doma’s martial art? As you know, Master, this is a matter of utmost importance.”

Master Seong spoke firmly.

“I am certain.”

There was not a shred of hesitation in Master Seong. With a confident expression, he gave weight to his assurance.

“Even if the Demon Cult Leader were here instead of the Demon Cult’s Young Cult Leader, I would give the same answer. I can say this on my name.”

His certainty left Jin Hagun confused. At first, when he had said it was demonic art, Jin Hagun had thought the ones who plotted this incident must have recruited an expert who used demonic arts to commit the deed.

But a Demon Venerable’s martial art?

‘Can someone forge a Demon Venerable’s demonic art?’

It seemed impossible.

‘What if it truly was Hyeolcheon Doma’s doing?’

Jin Hagun looked at Geom Mugeuk.

What on earth was he supposed to say to Geom Mugeuk in this situation? He could not say he trusted Master Seong’s eye, nor could he say in front of Master Seong that he trusted Geom Mugeuk.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk asked Jin Hagun,

“If evidence appeared that the one who did this was the Murim Alliance Leader, would you believe that evidence?”

It was such an absurd hypothetical that Jin Hagun did not even answer.

“You would never believe it, right? That is exactly how I feel right now.”

Geom Mugeuk stared intently at Master Seong.

“If he is not falsifying the result, then I’ll have to find out how they created this result.”

Master Seong spoke firmly.

“I did not falsify anything. Whether you believe me or not is not important.”

Geom Mugeuk nodded silently.

“That had better be the case. I don’t know who it is, but they keep touching things that must never be touched. They enraged my father, and now they have enraged me.”

It also meant that the enemy knew his father and him well.

In the meantime, the warriors of the Chongqing branch flooded into the main hall and surrounded Geom Mugeuk in layers. Thinking an incident had occurred, even the warriors of the Demon-Extermination Unit entered and watched the situation. Fortunately, nothing had happened to Jin Hagun.

Geom Mugeuk said to Jin Hagun,

“I should be going now.”

Jin Hagun could not find in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes any trace of panic at having his conspiracy exposed. His eyes were as clear and deep as ever.

“You will have to uncover the truth as quickly as possible.”

Jin Hagun believed Geom Mugeuk. He did not believe in the Demon Cult, did not believe in demonic practitioners, and did not believe in Hyeolcheon Doma, but he believed in his friend.

“I will. Thank you.”

When Geom Mugeuk tried to leave, Ju Gwol blocked him. The branch warriors raised their internal energy and pointed their swords at him in unison.

“Now that the martial art used by the culprit has been revealed to be demonic art, we cannot let you leave like this.”

If this incident was indeed the Demon Cult’s doing, he thought this was the perfect opportunity to capture the Demon Cult’s Young Cult Leader.

Then Jin Hagun said to him,

“Even if you want to capture him, we cannot do it with just us here. So let him go.”

Ju Gwol was not the only one surprised by those words. The warriors of the Chongqing branch present all thought the same thing.

‘Leaving us aside, Captain Jin and the Demon-Extermination Unit are here, and we still can’t capture a single Young Cult Leader?’

At that moment, Ju Gwol gravely misunderstood.

‘You’re going easy on him because he’s your friend. Listen, Captain Jin. This matter may drag you down from your position as successor.’

Jin Hagun looked at Ju Gwol and said,

“Open the way.”

Jin Hagun’s gaze was resolute, leaving no room for further obstinacy.

When Ju Gwol gave the order to his subordinates, only then did the branch warriors withdraw their swords in unison and open a path.

Geom Mugeuk quietly walked out between the Murim Alliance warriors.

Jin Hagun stood by the window and watched Geom Mugeuk cross the courtyard and leave the building.

From Ju Gwol and the branch warriors staring at his back, he sensed anger and agitation.

There were not many who would understand a friendship between the grandson of the Murim Alliance Leader, a leading candidate for successor, and the Demon Cult’s Young Cult Leader.

Moreover, Master Seong was someone everyone trusted when it came to demonic arts.

Judging from how things were going, this result too would quickly spread as rumor.

‘My friend, there is not much time.’

* * *

When I returned to the safe house, Father was in the courtyard, practicing qi circulation and breathing.

Lately, Father had been devoting himself noticeably to training his internal cultivation method.

I held my breath and waited for Father to finish his cultivation.

And at the very moment Father completed his qi circulation, I saw it: the true qi coiling around his body gathering together like a mirage in scorching heat.

It was the Heavenly Demon Soul.

For an instant, I had the illusion that the Heavenly Demon Soul formed of true qi was looking at me.

It was a frightening sight no matter how many times I saw it, but I was glad to see the Heavenly Demon Soul again after so long.

That gladness lasted only a moment. The Heavenly Demon Soul dissolved back into true qi, then scattered and was absorbed into Father’s body.

When Father slowly opened his eyes, their gaze was as deep as an abyss.

“Did you see it?”

“Yes, I saw it.”

Father’s gaze grew intense.

“The true qi of the Nineflame Demonic Art can only be seen by those who have learned it, and the form of the Heavenly Demon Soul only begins to appear faintly once one’s attainment reaches the seventh level. It means your cultivation has reached the seventh level.”

Father looked surprised that I had already reached the seventh level.

“Far faster than the achievement I made.”

And Father must have been called one of the fastest achievers in history.

Considering when I had begun learning the Nineflame Demonic Art, it was an absurdly fast achievement.

Of course, this achievement was only natural. On top of Father’s repeated lectures, I had been spending even more time cultivating through the Time-Heaven Secret Art.

“It is thanks to how well you taught me, Father.”

My achievement was surely stimulating Father.

I wanted to reach great completion as soon as possible and summon the Heavenly Demon Soul. I was unbearably curious how Father’s Heavenly Demon Soul and mine would differ.

“How did the matter you went to investigate turn out?”

“It was as you expected, Father.”

Before contacting the Martial Alliance Leader, Father had told me to first go and confirm what martial art had struck the Daejeong Sect. The reason he had me confirm it was because he thought they might have been attacked by demonic arts as well.

“What demonic art was it?”

Father clearly assumed it would be a known demonic art. Only then could they pin the blame on us with certainty.

I relayed a truth that even Father could not help but be shocked by.

“It was Elder Saber Demon’s martial art.”

Father’s gaze settled coldly. They had touched the Demonic Path, and they had touched a Demon Venerable, so Father’s anger was only natural.

“I don’t know how they fabricated it, but since Teacher Seong, who is well-versed in our cult’s demonic arts, uncovered it, the Martial Alliance will demand an explanation for this incident. Of course, Elder Saber Demon will be in our cult reading books and teaching Daeryong, so there shouldn’t be any problem.”

Just then, someone entered the safe house, guided by one of its warriors.

I had never imagined he would come, so I stared at him in surprise.

Seo Daeryong, carrying a great saber on his back, was stepping into the safe house.

Absolute Regression Chapter 498

Chapter 498: From Now On I Am the Murderer
------------------------------------------
Seo Daeryong ran up before Geom Ujin and paid his respects with utmost courtesy.

“I greet the Cult Leader.”

Geom Ujin accepted Seo Daeryong’s greeting.

“It has been a while.”

His father seemed slightly surprised by his appearance as well.

If it was Seo Daeryong, the Pavilion Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion, there was no problem. The problem was that he was the foremost disciple who had inherited Hyeolcheon Doma’s martial arts.

“I greet the Young Cult Leader.”

Since Geom Ujin was present, Seo Daeryong also formally paid his respects to Geom Mugeuk. Under normal circumstances, since this was a reunion after so long, he would have run over shouting, “I missed you, Young Cult Leader!”

At any other time, Geom Mugeuk would have accepted his greeting with a joke, but now was not the time for that. And above all, he had to uphold the authority of the Pavilion Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion in front of his father. The pleasure of joking around would last only a moment, but this moment before his father would affect Seo Daeryong’s life from here on.

“What brings the Pavilion Lord of Hwangcheon here?”

Seo Daeryong felt it. Not only Geom Mugeuk, but even Geom Ujin had been surprised by his appearance.

Seo Daeryong wanted to make a face and say this.

—Am I not supposed to be here?

But he could not possibly speak that way in front of the Cult Leader.

Geom Mugeuk read that thought from Seo Daeryong’s expression.

“We are in a situation where we should not be meeting Pavilion Lord Seo here right now.”

“What do you mean?”

“Right now, Father and I are with the culprit who destroyed Yumyeong Manor and the Daejeong Sect.”

Realizing that this culprit referred to him, Seo Daeryong’s eyes widened in shock.

“Me?”

“Yes. At the moment, you are the most likely suspect.”

Then unexpected words came from Seo Daeryong’s mouth.

“But how did you know? That I went there.”

This time, Geom Mugeuk was the one who was surprised.

“You went there?”

“Yes, I did. I went to both places.”

It was not even just one of them. Unable to hold back, Geom Mugeuk shouted.

“So Pavilion Lord Seo was the culprit!”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Seo Daeryong nearly jumped out of his skin.

“No, what are you—”

Seo Daeryong started to react as he normally would, then continued speaking politely.

“How could I be the culprit? It was not me!”

Of course, it had been Geom Mugeuk’s joke. Seo Daeryong was not the kind of person who would massacre an entire clan under any circumstances. It was said that even cooks who knew no martial arts and the women who worked there had all been killed. Geom Mugeuk knew well that neither Seo Daeryong nor Hyeolcheon Doma would ever commit such a brutal act.

Geom Mugeuk asked seriously again.

“Why did you come to Chongqing?”

The answer he had hoped would not come came. He wished Hyeolcheon Doma had simply stayed at the cult reading books.

“I came to see my master.”

Geom Ujin’s gaze, which had been listening, also sharpened.

He had wondered when Seo Daeryong appeared. If even Hyeolcheon Doma was here, this was no ordinary situation.

“Surely not. Is Elder Doma here?”

Seo Daeryong nodded and answered.

“Yes, he is here.”

“Tell me in detail what happened.”

Seo Daeryong glanced once at Geom Mugeuk, then began to recount what had happened in a trembling voice. He spoke to Geom Mugeuk, but it was a report to the Cult Leader.

“Some time ago, Master left the cult, saying he had something to attend to.”

“Without saying what it was?”

“Yes. He only said there was somewhere he had to go. But he was somewhat different from usual.”

“In what way?”

Because Hyeolcheon Doma was so strict when teaching martial arts, it had not been easy for Seo Daeryong to read his master’s feelings or inner thoughts.

But recently, the bond between them had deepened, and at times Hyeolcheon Doma even accepted Seo Daeryong’s jokes. Because of that, he could sense his master’s emotions better than before.

“Outwardly, he looked calm, but for some reason, his expression seemed dark.”

At those words, Geom Mugeuk’s expression naturally hardened. Seo Daeryong was quick-witted. If it was anyone else, maybe not, but if it was Hyeolcheon Doma, what he had seen was likely accurate. Something had happened to Hyeolcheon Doma?

“But I could not ask any further.”

Of course he could not. If Hyeolcheon Doma, of all people, said he had something to do and was leaving the cult, how could he have pressed him with questions?

“Then one day, long after he left the cult, this arrived for me.”

As he said that, Seo Daeryong took something out from his robes.

What he handed over was a book.

“Could this be?”

“That is right. It is the collection of poems and paintings Master usually enjoyed reading.”

This collection was certainly Hyeolcheon Doma’s book. On the front page remained the words Geom Mugeuk had left when returning it after borrowing it.

—If you had not become a martial artist, Elder, you would have become a poet.

“This arrived?”

Geom Mugeuk’s expression had grown grave.

“And this was tucked between the pages.”

Seo Daeryong handed over another sheet of paper.

—On such-and-such day, Chongqing, Yumyeong Manor.

It was a message to meet at the destroyed Yumyeong Manor.

Geom Mugeuk looked at his father in shock. It was clearly no ordinary matter. If there was a need to contact Seo Daeryong, a written message could have been sent; there was no reason to send the book. As expected, his father’s eyes had sunk coldly.

“Is it the elder’s handwriting?”

“I was not certain either. It seemed similar, and yet it did not. So I was going to have the handwriting specialist of my pavilion investigate it. But then I changed my mind and departed immediately.”

“Why did you leave without seeing the result?”

“When I thought about it, there was no need to confirm it. If it was Master’s handwriting, I naturally had to come. If it was not Master’s handwriting, then I had to come even faster.”

If someone else had written and sent it, that meant Hyeolcheon Doma was in danger.

“If you had been there, Young Cult Leader, I would have consulted you.”

But Geom Mugeuk was not there, nor was Ian. On top of that, Jang Homa had happened to leave the cult with the demonic troops.

In the end, Seo Daeryong came to Chongqing alone.

Geom Mugeuk realized it. This affair was a scheme by someone who knew Seo Daeryong would definitely come all the way to Chongqing. The enemy was someone who knew the circumstances of the cult and Hyeolcheon Doma well.

“I went to Yumyeong Manor on the date written there, but Master was not there. I did not know if the date had been written incorrectly or the place had been written incorrectly. Still, I could not simply return, so I waited at a nearby inn. I had a bad feeling. So I immediately relayed the situation to the Chief Strategist through Tongcheon Pavilion.”

“You did well.”

As expected, Seo Daeryong was clever.

“As I was waiting, that night someone sent a letter to the room where I was staying.”

Geom Mugeuk guessed the contents of the letter exactly.

“Was it a message to meet at the Daejeong Sect?”

“That is right. Since I had already found nothing the first time, I could not help but go. But this time too, it was for nothing. And then, starting the next day, those incidents happened one after another.”

When we turned the carriage around, Seo Daeryong had already come here and was already entangled in these incidents.

“Chongqing was sealed off because of the Yumyeong Manor and Daejeong Sect incidents. If I had really insisted on leaving, I could have, but I could not return without confirming Master’s safety. Then a message came from Tongcheon Pavilion. It said the Cult Leader and the Young Cult Leader were at the safe house, and that I should go there.”

If the Chief Strategist had not granted special permission, even the Pavilion Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion could not have come to a safe house reserved for the Heavenly Demon. In this urgent situation, Strategist Sama had made the wisest choice.

Seo Daeryong asked Geom Mugeuk.

“But what did you mean earlier? Why am I the culprit? Is it because I went to those places?”

But earlier, Geom Mugeuk had brought up the culprit before Seo Daeryong said he had gone there.

Geom Mugeuk told him the reason.

“The martial art used to kill the Daejeong Sect Leader was the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form.”

Seo Daeryong, startled, opened his eyes wide.

“That is impossible!”

That was why. Geom Mugeuk turned to his father and said,

“Whoever it is, they have plotted this rather convincingly.”

He could feel his father’s anger.

Rather, if someone had challenged his father directly, he would not have been this furious.

But they had touched the demonic path by leaving behind the words “Demonic Dao Under Heaven,” and now they had touched his father’s Demon Venerable.

“Before being my Hyeolcheon Doma, the Blade Demon is Father’s Demon Venerable.”

Geom Mugeuk could feel it. His father would never forgive the one behind this affair.

At last, Geom Ujin spoke in a low voice.

“He is not someone who would be defeated anywhere, so there is no need to worry about him.”

Those words revealed his absolute trust in Hyeolcheon Doma.

After saying that, his father went inside the building.

His father’s back was saying it: Find out where that man is, quickly.

Geom Mugeuk spoke loudly to his father.

“I will find out where the elder is.”

Once Geom Ujin entered his quarters, only Geom Mugeuk and Seo Daeryong remained in the courtyard of the safe house.

Only then did Seo Daeryong let out a sigh of relief. He had been very nervous before the Heavenly Demon. He had tried hard not to show it outwardly, but he had trembled a great deal.

“I was scared the Cult Leader would interrogate me, asking, ‘Was this not your doing?’”

Only now did the two of them begin speaking comfortably.

“As if that would happen. Father knows well enough that you are not the sort of person to commit such an act.”

“He knows about me?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“Do you think he simply handed you the position of Pavilion Lord of Hwangcheon just because I recommended you? The Chief Strategist has probably reported everything about Seo Daeryong the man.”

“Then he must know about my timidity too.”

Separate from the gravity of the situation, it was good to see Seo Daeryong after so long. A good person was good to see no matter when or under what circumstances.

“Have you been well?”

“Yes, I have been well.”

You do not know, Young Cult Leader, how much I missed you. I checked every day to see if you had returned.

“Did you take care of the matter you went for?”

At Seo Daeryong’s question, Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“Well, of course. With who went, how could it not have ended well?”

Even if Geom Mugeuk had gone alone, he could trust him; but the Cult Leader had gone with him as well.

“Even while we were handling matters there, I did not know the real problem was waiting here in Chongqing.”

Seo Daeryong asked carefully.

“Master will be all right, won’t he?”

But soon he looked down at the collection of poems and paintings and shook his head.

“There is no way he is all right when this is in our hands.”

After sinking into despair like that, he suddenly startled himself and shouted,

“What nonsense am I saying right now! The Cult Leader said so too, didn’t he? Our master is not someone who would be easily defeated anywhere. He will be all right.”

The deeper one’s affection grew, the more one’s worries grew as well.

When he was alone, he had somehow endured it, but when he saw Geom Mugeuk, the worry he had been holding back burst out. Seo Daeryong had been so worried about Hyeolcheon Doma that ever since this matter broke out, he had not slept properly.

Geom Mugeuk reassured Seo Daeryong.

“The elder will be all right.”

“You truly think so?”

“When have my words ever been wrong?”

“Of course, they have never been wrong!”

Geom Mugeuk thought of Hyeolcheon Doma.

The image that came to mind now was not of him swinging his enormous blade, nor his uncompromising gaze, nor his rigid, prickly personality.

The image that came to mind was of him sitting quietly and reading a book.

That was why he believed. Wherever he was, whoever faced him, he would not be easily defeated.

Because nowhere else could one find a Demon Venerable who read books.

Geom Mugeuk tucked the book into his robes. He was certain this book would protect Hyeolcheon Doma.

“Since this concerns the elder, you and I should resolve it, shouldn’t we?”

Flames rose in Seo Daeryong’s eyes.

“What should we do? Just leave anything to me.”

“They tried to make you the culprit of this affair. Witnesses who saw you will probably appear.”

A person who had learned the martial art that killed the Daejeong Sect Leader had visited the place beforehand? It was a situation with almost no way out. In that case?

“Let us move according to their intentions.”

“How?”

“When else in your life will you get to become such a villain?”

He meant that Seo Daeryong should become the villain who massacred the Daejeong Sect. Geom Mugeuk had expected him to shy away, asking if they really had to do that much, but Seo Daeryong answered readily.

“From this moment on, I am the culprit! Be careful. I am a merciless and terrifying fellow!”

“You will do it? If one wrong move is made, you may be branded by the martial world as that kind of villain, and this affair may end like that. Are you not afraid?”

“I am afraid.”

Seo Daeryong spoke honestly.

“You know, don’t you? How cowardly and timid I am. How much I look after my own skin. And how much I care about how others see me.”

But the reason he could overcome all of that was this.

“Still, I must do it. It concerns my master. Even if I die, I must do it.”

Seo Daeryong remembered the moment he first met Geom Mugeuk. He remembered how dark, gloomy, and timid he had been back then.

He also remembered the first time he met Hyeolcheon Doma. That day when, drunk, he clung to the hem of his trousers. He remembered Hyeolcheon Doma, who had put that version of him to bed. Despite being someone with such a fastidious nature.

When he thought of that time, what was there to hesitate over?

“It is unfortunate. That I can only serve as bait.”

How could Geom Mugeuk not know his heart? His desire to play a splendid role and resolve this affair. His yearning to be acknowledged by Hyeolcheon Doma.

But it was still too much for him. The one behind this affair was no ordinary bastard. Even serving as bait would not be easy.

“You may be bait, but you are bait to catch a monster. The pinnacle of bait-kind, a clever bait who entered at the top of the class, the greatest bait in the martial world.”

Seo Daeryong smiled faintly. Truly, if not for this Young Cult Leader before his eyes, he would already have fainted several times in this situation. But instead of running away and turning his back, he was saying this.

“That bait, even if it gets swallowed into the monster’s belly, you must pull it back out!”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 499

Chapter 499: Seven Eight and Nine Are All Here.
-----------------------------------------------
Jin Ha-gun gazed out the window of the Zhongjing Branch Leader's office.

Martial artists were filing into the training grounds. They were martial artists who had come as support from the Martial Alliance's Shaanxi Branch. From every branch and sub-branch adjacent to Zhongjing, martial artists were steadily gathering.

Having already heard word of the situation here, their expressions were stiff. They looked prepared for a battle with the Demonic Cult. And it was not only them.

Word had come that in every corner of Zhongjing, conflicts were breaking out between orthodox martial artists and demonic practitioners. Truly, the atmosphere was turbulent.

A short distance from Jin Ha-gun stood Branch Leader Ju Gweol.

"The Alliance Leader has granted you the authority to decide on the Net of Heaven and Earth, my lord."

Meaning, he was to judge as he saw fit.

Jin Ha-gun could sense it. That if he mishandled this matter, he might fail to become the successor.

His grandfather was not a man to forgive mistakes simply because he was his grandson. Rather, he would judge him by even stricter standards. Therefore, he had to judge and handle this well.

"If you wish to deploy the Net of Heaven and Earth, you must do so immediately."

The lifeblood of the Net of Heaven and Earth was timing. If it was deployed after the enemy had escaped, it would be utterly useless.

Ju Gweol's heart was urgent, but Jin Ha-gun's thoughts were different.

'Whether the culprit is the Blood Heaven Saber Demon or not.'

Jin Ha-gun's gaze turned beyond the distant wall.

'They are all here.'

Both the one plotting the conspiracy and the one caught in it.

'They have no intention of fleeing. They intend to see this through to the end, right here.'

If so, rather than deploying the Net of Heaven and Earth and scattering forces, keeping them concentrated in one place would make it easier to prepare for whatever happened next.

Just then, a subordinate entered the office and reported.

"We have found the informant who witnessed the culprit."

Jin Ha-gun and Ju Gweol were startled.

"Bring him here at once."

At Ju Gweol's command, the subordinate returned with a man.

The man entered with a face full of tension and fear; he was a street vendor who sold goods from a stall in the marketplace.

"The day before the massacre, there was a martial artist who asked where Daejeong Gate was."

Ju Gweol asked him,

"Do you remember what he looked like?"

"He was short and had a frail stature."

"His age? Was he an old man?"

"No. He was a young man."

At the word "young," Ju Gweol was inwardly disappointed. Because he had expected someone who had seen the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

"What weapon was he carrying?"

"He had a large saber strapped to his back."

At the mention of the large saber, life returned to Ju Gweol's eyes.

"Do you remember his face?"

"Yes, he had a unique face, so I remember."

Ju Gweol ordered the subordinate to take the man to a sketch artist immediately and have a face portrait drawn.

"It is a well-known fact that the Blood Heaven Saber Demon passed his martial arts to his head disciple. Perhaps the culprit could be that disciple."

Jin Ha-gun thought otherwise. Because the evidence falling into place so neatly was, rather, strange.

"Branch Leader, do you think someone going to annihilate a clan would ask for directions on the street?"

Ju Gweol had nothing to say to that, but he had something else to say.

"If that man is the Blood Heaven Saber Demon's head disciple. Do you think it could be a coincidence that a demonic practitioner who learned that martial art would happen to visit the place annihilated by that very art for some other business?"

Jin Ha-gun likewise found it difficult to refute.

If he were revealed to be the Blood Heaven Saber Demon's head disciple, everyone would consider this affair the work of the Demonic Cult. Because, as Ju Gweol said, such a coincidence could not occur.

"First, have as many copies drawn of the portrait the witness described and post them everywhere in Zhongjing. Finding him is the priority."

Separate from the faith he held in Geom Mugeuk, he could not dismiss the faint possibility that the Blood Heaven Saber Demon's disciple had committed this massacre.

"You must deploy the Net of Heaven and Earth."

"I will decide that myself. Focus on finding him for now."

"Understood."

Ju Gweol went outside.

Jin Ha-gun spoke to a subordinate of the Demon Extermination Corps.

"Send word to the Young Cult Leader. Tell him I wish to meet in secret."

* * *

Faint moonlight settled upon each blade of grass.

Geom Mugeuk stood alone in the wide-open field.

He was certain Seo Daeryong would be fine, but Geom Mugeuk worried about the Blood Heaven Saber Demon. He worried more because he knew how strong he was. Because all news from that powerful man had ceased.

'Elder.'

He missed him. He recalled the image of him planting the Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber into the ground and leaning on the blade as he drank.

'When this matter is over, let us lean on that saber together and share a drink, Elder.'

Then, a figure revealed himself there.

The one who slowly walked over and stood beside Geom Mugeuk was Jin Ha-gun.

"You're here?"

"Thank you for coming."

"If you call, I naturally must come. You must be busy; why did you call me?"

At that, Jin Ha-gun took a sheet of paper from his robes and handed it to Geom Mugeuk.

It was a drawing with a person's face portrait sketched on it.

"Was it drawn to look like him?"

To Jin Ha-gun's question, Geom Mugeuk answered frankly.

"It's exactly the same. It looks as though he sat right in front and was drawn."

Those words also constituted an admission that the figure in the drawing was the Blood Heaven Saber Demon's disciple.

"Who reported him?"

"They say that person asked a street merchant where Daejeong Gate was."

"He must deal with hundreds of people a day; isn't it strange that he remembered the face so accurately like this?"

And above all, it had truly captured Seo Daeryong's features well. Unless this was prepared in advance, such a drawing could not have been produced.

"Fabricated?"

"The informant has likely already disappeared, or been killed."

Since he had already finished testifying and even described a portrait, nothing would change. If people believed the witness had been killed to silence him, the hatred toward the Demonic Cult would only grow stronger.

"I'll look into it."

A thought arose in a corner of Jin Ha-gun's mind.

'Could you have removed him yourself?'

To deceive me in turn.

Feeling such a thought still arise, he realized he did not yet fully trust him.

"Where is the Blood Heaven Saber Demon's disciple now?"

"He is with me."

Jin Ha-gun spoke, looking at the drawing in Geom Mugeuk's hand.

"From tomorrow, that drawing will be posted everywhere in Zhongjing. Rumors will even spread that the culprit is the Blood Heaven Saber Demon's head disciple."

Geom Mugeuk looked at Jin Ha-gun.

"You must be in a difficult position."

"Difficult" was not enough. He was the one responsible for finding that culprit, and his grandfather would decide whether to make him the successor or not depending on how he resolved this matter.

—Hand that person over to me.

This thought rose to his throat. He wanted to investigate directly and be certain.

Right now, the faith he held in Geom Mugeuk was nine out of ten. That remaining one kept tormenting him.

"If your people arrest him, he won't be able to get free. He has mastered the Heaven-Extinguishing Demonic Saber Technique that killed the Daejeong Gate Lord, and he even visited Daejeong Gate the day before. But, do you know this?"

Geom Mugeuk said with a faint smile.

"This man was the top-ranked entrant at Hwangcheon Gak. He may look gloomy, but he is that smart. He is not the kind of person to leave evidence trailing everywhere while annihilating a die-hard orthodox sect."

Jin Ha-gun thought so too, but…

"But in the end, the revealed evidence inevitably becomes the truth. If it is for that head disciple's sake, you must find the real culprit. And as quickly as possible."

The two stood silently for a moment.

A gust of wind blew, and the grass swayed like waves; from afar, the sound of insects chirping could be heard.

"What do you think they are aiming for by going this far?"

To Jin Ha-gun's question, Geom Mugeuk answered calmly.

"War between you and us."

Jin Ha-gun was startled. He had assumed those bastards were trying to achieve some purpose through sowing discord; the thought that the purpose was a Righteous-Demonic Great War had never once crossed his mind.

Jin Ha-gun looked at Geom Mugeuk with a gaze asking if he was serious.

And Geom Mugeuk was serious.

"I think these incidents are merely the beginning. After intensifying the conflict between us like this, they will cause another incident. I don't know what their plan is, but by then it will be irreversible."

These horrific incidents were merely the beginning?

"By starting a war?"

There had to be something they stood to gain to attempt such a massive undertaking.

"That I don't know either. What it is they want."

At first, Geom Mugeuk had thought it might be the Blood Heaven Saber Demon's personal grudge.

But if that were the case, they were making this matter too large. Moreover, they had roped in Seo Daeryong as well.

'There is something more.'

Jin Ha-gun let out a light sigh.

"This is difficult."

Amid the difficulty, there was hope that Geom Mugeuk had found.

"But there is one fact those bastards have overlooked."

"What is it?"

"You and me."

Geom Mugeuk looked at Jin Ha-gun.

"The fact that we are closer friends than they think."

At Geom Mugeuk's gaze directed at him, Jin Ha-gun averted his eyes. He felt that now was the time he had to say this.

"I only trust you nine out of ten. Are we still friends?"

Jin Ha-gun mustered his courage. It was the part that had always tormented him. The fact that while Geom Mugeuk thought of him as a friend, he could not fully trust him.

Then, an unexpected answer flowed out.

"You trust me nine out of ten? I only trust you eight out of ten."

He wondered if Geom Mugeuk was joking again, but Geom Mugeuk was serious.

"You really are meant to become a great hero. To think you consider trusting someone nine out of ten as lacking. Look here, friend. You are the Lord of the Demon Extermination Corps, and I am the Young Cult Leader of the Divine Cult. And you say you trust me nine out of ten?"

To Jin Ha-gun, the word "trust" was something that could only be used when one trusted fully, all ten out of ten.

"Let's go with a relationship of about eight out of ten. We put our lives on the line earnestly for that eight. But let's leave the remaining two as leeway. We are human too—shouldn't we sometimes doubt and even hate each other?"

Jin Ha-gun stared silently at Geom Mugeuk. Despite such words, "doubt" and "hate" did not suit him at all.

"What if those remaining two betray us?"

"How can two betray us? The remaining eight are still there."

Jin Ha-gun had never once thought of trust between people in this way. Because he had believed that the one part he could not fill would betray him.

"If the eight are firm, those two's rebellion won't be betrayal but mere hurt feelings. If the eight are firm, we can free ourselves from the burden of having to trust someone completely. Besides, what is harder than trusting is giving trust, is it not? Complete trust? Let's not live such a tiresome life. Let's live making mistakes and being hated moderately. Trusting eight firmly is enough."

Jin Ha-gun did not know.

Did Geom Mugeuk know that he had worried over this matter for a long time? If not, how could he put a person's heart so at ease? How on earth?

"Then we are friends."

"Of course. In that sense, would you care to take a look at some bait?"

"Bait?"

Geom Mugeuk looked behind him. Then, from behind a distant tree, a man walked this way.

Jin Ha-gun realized the person who arrived there was Seo Daeryong from the drawing. He was inwardly surprised that Geom Mugeuk had brought him here.

Instead of introducing them, Geom Mugeuk asked Seo Daeryong,

"Out of ten in work, how much do you trust me?"

What was the point of asking? It was obviously ten. He was the person he trusted most in this world.

Of course, if he answered straight at an opportunity to joke like this, he would not be Seo Daeryong.

"Wouldn't it be about seven?"

Geom Mugeuk said to Jin Ha-gun.

"My right-hand man says seven."

"We started from one at our first meeting, so it's remarkable progress. Honestly, back then I didn't even trust you one bit."

A Young Cult Leader who says he'll change the cult?

Geom Mugeuk handed the paper in his hand to Seo Daeryong.

"Here, keep this as a memento."

And then he said to Jin Ha-gun,

"We don't need to spread this drawing tomorrow."

Jin Ha-gun was startled by Geom Mugeuk's words. He realized it meant Geom Mugeuk was entrusting Seo Daeryong to him.

"Are you serious?"

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

"The reason I'm entrusting this man to you is not because he is the culprit. This man is my right arm. If this man is the culprit, then so am I."

He trusted him this much.

"Why would you do this, knowing he cannot get out easily?"

The reason for entrusting him was not something Jin Ha-gun had expected from Geom Mugeuk.

"I'm thinking of striking at their opening."

"Their opening? What do you mean?"

"They must have expected that I would never hand over my right arm. Because they know the relationship between me and this man. But what if I hand him over to you?"

He was trying to throw bait with an unexpected move.

He intended to find a clue to track them from their reaction and response.

"I've become curious. If they drew in the Saber Demon, why did they drag his disciple in as well? What role does my right arm play in their plan?"

Was his role simply to cause strife between the main cult and the Martial Alliance? Or was there another reason?

Geom Mugeuk said to Jin Ha-gun,

"In the process of solving that curiosity, you may be in danger."

Because there was no telling how they would try to deal with Seo Daeryong captured by the Martial Alliance. It meant that simply being with Seo Daeryong could put him in danger.

Though warned of the danger, Jin Ha-gun instead gave a faint smile.

"Seven is boldly stepping forward like this; am I not nine?"

Since his joke was truly rare, Geom Mugeuk welcomed it gladly.

"Then seven, eight, and nine have all gathered here."

The three laughed together.

After easing the tension like this, Geom Mugeuk looked at Seo Daeryong and said,

"Villain, now is the time for your punishment."

Absolute Regression — Episode 500.

Chapter 500: If You Know Who's Coming.
--------------------------------------
The Demon Extermination Squad, led by Jinhagun, entered the Chongqing Branch of the Wulin Alliance.

This was the sight that met Jugwol’s eyes when he came running after hearing the news.

Eight Demon Extermination Squad warriors were entering the training yard, surrounding a single man in their midst.

Jugwol realized that the man being dragged along at the center was the man from the portrait.

“You caught him!”

The Wulin Alliance warriors who had been watching erupted in a collective cheer of joy.

A moment later, killing intent poured toward Seo Daeryong. If not for Jinhagun and the Demon Extermination Squad, they would have showered him with curses and jeers.

Jugwol ran toward Jinhagun. The look in his eyes softened considerably when he saw him.

“Well done.”

He had been worried that Jinhagun might go easy on the demonic cultivators because Geom Mugeuk was his friend, but Jinhagun had brought Seo Daeryong in himself.

‘This is how one properly separates public and private matters.’

In truth, Jugwol was no better at separating public and private matters.

These incidents had taken place in Chongqing, which was under his responsibility, and he was naturally acquainted with both the Lord of Famous Manor and the Sect Master of Great Justice Sect. As a result, his personal feelings had played a major role in this affair as well.

Jugwol glared at Seo Daeryong with terrifying eyes.

But Seo Daeryong pretended not to notice and only stared at the back of the warrior walking in front of him.

‘Young Cult Master, I’ve truly had every experience imaginable in my life.’

He had won the Little Dragon Tournament at the Wulin Alliance and heard cheers of celebration. This time, he was receiving an intense killing intent filled with hatred.

The place they entered was the interrogation room of the Chongqing Branch.

The man who had once interrogated criminals in the Yellow Springs Pavilion’s interrogation rooms now sat here as a criminal himself.

Seo Daeryong let out a quiet sigh and looked around. Though he was acting as bait for the operation, now that he had actually entered a Wulin Alliance interrogation room, he felt tense and afraid.

From this point on, he had to keep his wits about him. The Demon Extermination Squad was guarding him as tightly as an iron wall for now, but there was no telling when someone might seize an opening and try to kill him.

The first person to enter was Jinhagun.

“Are you thirsty?”

“I’m fine.”

Seo Daeryong answered Jinhagun politely.

Strictly speaking, if one went by rank alone, Seo Daeryong’s position as a Pavilion Master and Squad Leader was actually higher. Even so, he showed Jinhagun due courtesy because Jinhagun was a leading candidate for the next Alliance Leader—and, above all, because he was the Young Cult Master’s friend.

“Thank you for your consideration.”

Jinhagun was the one who ought to be grateful. Word would spread that he had captured the culprit, which would greatly enhance his reputation.

“Are you afraid?”

“The Young Cult Master gave me his word. He said that even if I were swallowed by a monster, he would pull me back out. So all I need to do is play the role of being swallowed deliciously.”

If he had heard such words before learning what kind of person Geom Mugeuk was, he would have laughed inwardly.

You’re being used.

But now, Seo Daeryong made him feel envious. And that led him to a conclusion: No matter how he looked at it, those eyes were not the eyes of a man worth only seven points.

“The interrogators will be coming in shortly. They’ll do whatever it takes to obtain a confession from you.”

“I’m prepared.”

In fact, Seo Daeryong was more worried about Jinhagun.

“Squad Leader, you should avoid staying with me if possible.”

“Are you worried about me?”

“There’s no need for you to remain with me in a situation that could become dangerous, is there?”

Seo Daeryong was not worried about Jinhagun. He was worried about the Young Cult Master. He knew how deeply the man would grieve if he lost a friend.

Just then, the interrogators entered the room.

Jinhagun said to them,

“Get to the bottom of the truth thoroughly.”

“Yes, sir.”

Jinhagun glanced once at Seo Daeryong before leaving.

Branch Lord Jugwol was waiting outside the door.

“He is the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s direct disciple. The Demon Venerable may come to rescue him, so make sure the branch’s defenses are fully prepared.”

“He wouldn’t dare think of entering this branch.”

Reinforcements from the nearby branches, led by the Shaanxi Branch, were arriving one after another.

“That’s why the Branch Lord mustn’t let his guard down even for a moment.”

Jugwol immediately realized his mistake and answered respectfully.

“I won’t let my guard down. I’ll guard the branch well.”

Even so, he was certain that they would not be able to attack this place.

Jinhagun left the building.

He had joined hands with Geom Mugeuk and made a move that defied the enemy’s expectations.

‘Now, how will you respond?’

* * *

In a corner of Chongqing’s marketplace, an old fortune-teller sat alone. His face was covered in wrinkles that spoke of the hardships of the years, and he watched passersby with eyes that seemed to know every secret of life.

Spread across the mat before him were all kinds of objects used for divination: flickering candles and incense, a tortoiseshell painted in eerie colors, divination coins specially made for fortune-telling, talismans inscribed in red, and bamboo lots filling a wooden container. Every conceivable object was laid out before him.

Many passersby crossed in front of him, but no one paid him any attention. The old fortune-teller appeared equally content whether he made a sale or not.

Then, at last, a customer arrived.

A man passing by crouched down before him. He had an ordinary appearance and wore ordinary clothes.

The old fortune-teller examined the man’s face and said,

“Your face is filled with misfortune.”

“I could have guessed that much myself. If things were going well for me, would I even be looking over here?”

Passersby who overheard him laughed.

“Let’s see just how badly things are going for you.”
